18 ANTI-DISCRIMINATION & HARASSMENT POLICIES
T3TG is committed to providing a work environment that is free from unlawful discrimination and
harassment in any form. It is T3TG’s policy to comply with all applicable laws that provide equal
opportunity in employment for all persons and to prohibit unlawful discrimination in employment. T3TG’s
policy is not to discriminate by reason of race, color, religion, sex/gender, gender identity, sexual
orientation, marital status, pregnancy, genetic information, citizenship status, national origin, age,
disability or veteran status. T3Tg Policy prohibits unlawful discrimination/harassment and other
inappropriate or unprofessional conduct.

                                 18.1 T3TG Policy
This policy applies to all terms and conditions of employment, including, but not limited to, hiring,
placement, promotion, termination, layoff, recall, transfer, leaves of absence, compensation, and
training.

18.1.1 Accommodations
T3TG will make reasonable accommodations for qualified individuals with disabilities unless doing so
would result in an undue hardship. Persons with disabilities who are in need of accommodation are
encouraged to discuss their needs with their Supervisor and the Compliance Department.

This policy applies to all terms and conditions of employment, including, but not limited to, hiring,
placement, promotion, termination, layoff, recall, transfer, leaves of absence, compensation, and
training.

18.1.2 Work Environment
T3TG insists that all individuals be treated with respect and dignity. Each individual should be able to work
in a professional atmosphere that promotes teamwork and attainment of T3TG’s goals. Improper
interference with the ability of T3TG’s RR/Members to perform their expected job duties is unacceptable
and unprofessional. T3TG expressly prohibits any form of unlawful discrimination, unlawful harassment,
and any other inappropriate or unprofessional conduct and will not tolerate, condone, or allow such
conduct by fellow RR/Members, supervisors, managers, vendors, contractors, visitors, or others who
conduct T3TG business. T3TG’s “work environment” extends to all T3TG facilities and any setting outside
the workplace where individuals who are associated with T3TG are gathered, such as business trips,
conferences, and social events.

18.1.3 Sexual Harassment Prevention
The purpose of this policy statement on sexual harassment is to establish clearly and unequivocally that
T3TG prohibits sexual harassment by and of its RR/Members. No associated person, either male or
female, should be subjected to unsolicited or unwelcome sexual advances or conduct either verbal or
physical. "Sexual harassment" has been defined by government agencies and the courts as conduct which
includes all unwelcome sexual advances, requests for sexual favors, and all other verbal or physical
conduct of a sexual nature when:



 Compliance and Supervisory Procedures                                                          Page 267
 T3TG Policy                                                                                   09062023
            •   Submission to such conduct is made either explicitly or implicitly a term or condition of
                employment;
            •   Submission to, or rejection of, such conduct by an individual is used as the basis for
                employment decisions affecting such individual; or
            •   Such conduct is sufficiently severe or pervasive to alter the conditions of employment and
                to create a hostile or abusive working environment.

Sexual harassment may take many forms, including, but not limited to: verbal teasing or abuse of a sexual
nature; inquiries about sexual activities, sexual orientation and gender identity; subtle pressure or abuse
of a sexual nature; inappropriate, unwelcome and unnecessary touching of an individual, for example,
grabbing, patting, pinching, hugging, repeatedly brushing against another associated person’s body or
impeding the movement of another person; offensive sexual flirtation, advances, or propositioning,
including suggestive looks or gestures; sexually graphic verbal commentaries or jokes made in any
medium; sexually degrading words used to describe an individual; or the offensive display in the
workplace of sexual or pornographic objects, pictures or writings.

18.1.4 Racial/Ethnic Discrimination and Harassment
The purpose of this policy statement on racial/ethnic discrimination and harassment is to establish clearly
and unequivocally that T3TG prohibits racial and ethnic discrimination, including racial/ethnic
harassment, by and of its RR/Members. No RR/Members, regardless of race or ethnicity, should be
subjected to racially or ethnically offensive, hostile, or intimidating conduct either verbal or physical.
Conduct with racial or ethnic content or overtones, which is severe, pervasive or persistent, may be
considered racial/ethnic harassment. Racial/ethnic harassment may take many forms, including, but not
limited to: menacing behavior, words, or symbols directed at persons of a particular race or ethnicity;
displaying, distributing or otherwise communicating anything offensive in any medium which focuses on
race or ethnicity; making critical or mocking comments about racial or ethnic characteristics, such as
voice, accent, physical features, hair texture, or manner of dress; perpetuating or promoting racial/ethnic
stereotypes, such as assumptions about likes and dislikes based on race or ethnicity; assigning persons to
an isolated or separate work area based on their race or ethnicity; basing overtime assignments on race
or ethnicity.

18.1.5 Other Illegal Harassment
In addition to sexual, racial, and ethnic harassment, T3TG recognizes that other forms of unlawful
harassment exist. Such unlawful harassment may target persons because of their color, religion, sexual
orientation, gender, marital status, pregnancy, genetic information, citizenship status, national origin,
age, disability, veteran status, or other factors. T3TG will not tolerate such conduct.

18.1.6 Other Inappropriate or Unprofessional Conduct
T3TG also prohibits conduct by or toward any RR/Members, supervisors, managers, vendors, contractors,
visitors, or others who conduct business with T3TG that is inappropriate in a work environment or is
considered unprofessional. Such conduct includes behaviors that improperly interfere with the ability of
T3TG’s RR/Members to perform their expected job duties.



 Compliance and Supervisory Procedures                                                        Page 268
 T3TG Policy                                                                                 09062023
18.1.7 Complaint Procedure
T3TG Investigates Complaints Relating to Any Unlawful Discrimination Or Harassment and to
Inappropriate or Unprofessional Behavior.

Each RR/Members of T3TG is responsible for maintaining a work atmosphere free of discrimination and
harassment, sexual or otherwise. Further, RR/Members of T3TG are responsible for respecting the rights
of fellow RR/Members. It is T3TG’s policy to thoroughly investigate and remedy any known incidence of
unlawful discrimination or harassment. T3TG is equally committed to respond to complaints of
inappropriate or unprofessional behavior. In order to accomplish this policy, unlawful discrimination or
harassment, as well as any other inappropriate or unprofessional behavior by or toward any T3TG
RR/Members, supervisor, manager, vendor, contractor, visitor, or other person, must be brought to the
attention of T3TG Compliance. Accordingly, if you experience any harassment based on your gender, race,
ethnicity, religion, or other Protected Classification, believe you have been treated in an unlawful,
discriminatory manner, or are subjected to inappropriate or unprofessional behavior, do not ignore it.
Although T3TG encourages you to express your concerns directly to the offending person, if, in your
judgment, informal, direct communications between individuals is ineffective or impossible, you should
immediately communicate your problem to T3TG Compliance. To do so, promptly report the incident to
Compliance@T3Trading.com or in person (ideally within 48 hours of the occurrence), who will investigate
the matter and take appropriate action.

18.1.8 No Retaliation
T3TG prohibits any form of retaliation against any RR/Members for making a bona fide complaint under
this policy or for assisting in a complaint investigation. Retaliation of any kind will be considered a violation
of this policy.

18.1.9 Remedial Measures
If unlawful discrimination, harassment, inappropriate or unprofessional conduct is found to have
occurred, appropriate remedial action will be taken. If a T3TG RR/Members is found to have violated this
policy, such RR/Members will be subject to disciplinary action up to and including immediate termination.

18.1.10 Management Responsibilities
Managers and supervisors have special responsibilities with respect to the policy against discrimination
and harassment. They must understand T3TG’s Policy prohibiting discrimination and harassment.
Generally, T3TG’s anti-discrimination policy requires that employment decisions and actions are made
without regard to a person’s race, color, religion, sex/gender, gender identity, sexual orientation, marital
status, pregnancy, genetic information, citizenship status, national origin, age, disability, or veteran
status. Examples of employment decisions include, but are not limited to, evaluating Associated Person’s
performance; making recommendations on hires, transfers, discipline, corrective action and termination.
Not only must managers and supervisors conduct themselves in a manner consistent with this policy, they
are also responsible for establishing and maintaining a work environment free of unlawful harassment
and unlawful discrimination. Managers and supervisors must educate RR/Members on the policy. They
must maintain open lines of communication, allowing RR/Members to express their concerns without
fear of retaliation. They must stop any form of harassment they observe. They must identify incidents of


 Compliance and Supervisory Procedures                                                             Page 269
 T3TG Policy                                                                                      09062023
discrimination immediately and report them to Compliance. Managers and supervisors must ensure, with
assistance from Compliance, that an investigation of allegations of discrimination or harassment is
initiated immediately after they are reported. Based on the investigation, managers and supervisors must
take appropriate and immediate action.

18.1.11 RR/Members Responsibility
RR/Members have important responsibilities with respect to the policy against unlawful discrimination
and harassment. T3TG’s RR/Members are responsible for knowing and understanding this policy and
promoting a workplace free of unlawful discrimination and harassment by conducting themselves in a
manner consistent with this policy. RR/Members also have a responsibility to report any unlawful
discrimination or harassing conduct they are subjected to or they observe taking place toward another
RR/Members immediately to their supervisor, or Compliance.

18.1.12 Compliance Responsibility
Compliance has the same responsibilities as all other RR/Members relative to compliance with T3TG
policy. In addition, they are responsible for conducting thorough investigations of the complaints that are
brought forward by management. Compliance should also notify T3TG management within twenty-four
hours of all complaints of discrimination and harassment. Compliance will assist in determining the proper
mode of investigation and the appropriateness of involving other Company resources. Maintaining
appropriate confidentiality throughout investigations is critical.3110
